/**
 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
 * 
 * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response
 * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
 * 
 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
 * 
 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
 */

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <random>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// ============================================================================
// Types and Constants
// ============================================================================

using idx_t = uint32_t;
using val_t = float;

constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;

// ============================================================================
// Vector and Triangle Types
// ============================================================================

struct Vec3 {
    val_t x, y, z;

    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}

    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }

    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    }

    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    Vec3 normalized() const {
        val_t n = norm();
        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    }

    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    }
};

struct Triangle {
    Vec3 a, b, c;
    Vec3 _normal;

    Triangle() = default;
    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}

    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }

    val_t area() const {
        Vec3 ab = b - a;
        Vec3 ac = c - a;
        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    }

    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
// ============================================================================

// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
    // Translate triangle to box center
    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;

    // Triangle edges
    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;
    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;

    // Test box normals (AABB axes)
    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {
        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));
    };

    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);
    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;

    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);
    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;

    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);
    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;

    // Test triangle normal
    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);
    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);
    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +
              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +
              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);
    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;

    // Test 9 edge cross products
    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {
        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);
        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);
        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);
        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +
                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +
                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
    };

    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};
    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};
    for (const auto& axis : axes) {
        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);
            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {
                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Octree for Spatial Acceleration
// ============================================================================

constexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;
constexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;

class Octree {
public:
    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;
    Vec3 halfExtent, center;
    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];
    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list
    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles

    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}

    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {
        allTriangles = &triangles;
        if (triangles.empty()) return;

        // Compute bounding box
        minBound = triangles[0].a;
        maxBound = triangles[0].a;
        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {
            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {
                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);
                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);
                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);
                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);
                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);
                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);
            }
        }

        // Collect all indices
        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;

        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);
    }

private:
    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {
        minBound = nodeMin;
        maxBound = nodeMax;
        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;
        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;

        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf
        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||
            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {
            triangleIndices = indices;
            return;
        }

        // Subdivide into 8 children
        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;
        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];

        for (size_t idx : indices) {
            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];

            // Check which children this triangle overlaps
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                Vec3 childCenter = center;
                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;
                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;
                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;

                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {
                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);
                }
            }
        }

        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)
        bool canSplit = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {
                canSplit = true;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!canSplit) {
            triangleIndices = indices;
            return;
        }

        // Build children
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {
                Vec3 childMin = center;
                Vec3 childMax = center;
                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;
                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;
                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;
                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;
                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;
                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;

                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();
                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;
                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);
            }
        }
    }

public:
    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box
    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {
        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;
        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;
        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};

        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;

        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;

        return true;
    }

    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray
    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle
    template<typename Func>
    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {
        // If leaf node, check triangles directly
        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {
            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {
                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        // Otherwise, descend to children
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {
                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Mesh Generation: Icosphere
// ============================================================================

// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron
class IcosphereMesh {
public:
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;

    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {
        // Initial icosahedron vertices
        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;

        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {
            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius
        };

        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)
        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {
            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},
            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},
            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},
            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}
        };

        // Subdivide
        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {
            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;
            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;

            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {
                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));
                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);
                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;

                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;
                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;
                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());
                vertices.push_back(mid);
                midpointCache[key] = idx;
                return idx;
            };

            for (const auto& face : faces) {
                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);
                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);
                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);

                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});
                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});
                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});
                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});
            }
            faces = std::move(newFaces);
        }

        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
        triangles.reserve(faces.size());
        for (const auto& face : faces) {
            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward
            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
        }
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Random Number Generation
// ============================================================================

class RandomGenerator {
    std::mt19937 rng;
    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
public:
    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
};

// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
    val_t u = rng.rand();
    val_t v = rng.rand();
    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
        u = 1.0f - u;
        v = 1.0f - v;
    }
    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
}

// ============================================================================
// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
// ============================================================================

val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);

    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
}

// ============================================================================
// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
// ============================================================================

// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
    Vec3 dir = to - from;
    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;

    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;

        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
            return true;  // Ray is blocked
        }
        return false;
    });
}

// ============================================================================
// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
// ============================================================================

// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
}

// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];

    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;

    val_t kij = ZERO;

    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);

        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;

        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;

        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());

        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;

        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
    }

    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
}

// ============================================================================
// Tau (time delay) Computation
// ============================================================================

int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
}

// ============================================================================
// Room Response Simulation State
// ============================================================================

struct SimulationState {
    size_t numTriangles;
    size_t numTimesteps;

    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source

    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

    size_t sourceIndex;

    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    // Generate mesh
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;

    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);

    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
    printf("Building octree...\n");
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    // Initialize areas
    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Initialize matrices
    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);

    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
    size_t timeOn = 0;
    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
    RandomGenerator rng(42);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
        }

        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);

    // Check source distance is zero or very small
    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
    }

    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
    int receivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                receivedEnergy++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);

    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)
    int nonZeroKij = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;
    }
    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));

    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    if (!allNonNegative) {
        return false;
    }

    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Convert distances to double for output
        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
        print_results(distData, "Distances");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        if (!validateResults(state)) {
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

def run(key, args, threads=1):
    env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_DYNAMIC=\"FALSE\", OMP_PROC_BIND=\"close\", OMP_PLACES=\"cores\")
    start = time.monotonic()

+            for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
+                RandomGenerator rowRng = rng;
+                size_t sampledPairs = 0;
+                const Vec3 normal = state.triangles[i].normal();
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {

+                        state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
+                            i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rowRng);
+                    }
+                    #pragma omp critical(roomsim_progress)
+                    {
+                        ++completed;
+                        if (completed % 100 == 0 || completed == state.numTriangles) {

+                    }

    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Initialize matrices
    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);

    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
    size_t timeOn = 0;
    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
    size_t completed = 0;

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp single
        {
            RandomGenerator rng(42);
            for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
                RandomGenerator rowRng = rng;
                size_t sampledPairs = 0;
                const Vec3 normal = state.triangles[i].normal();
                for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                    if (i != j && !(normal.dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) {
                        ++sampledPairs;
                    }
                }
                // Each uncullled pair consumes four float draws per ray, even
                // when a ray is blocked. A float draw uses one mt19937 output.
                // Advance the original stream while workers trace earlier rows.
                rng.discard(static_cast<uint64_t>(sampledPairs) * (4 * NUM_RAYS));

                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, rowRng) shared(state, completed)
                {
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                        if (i == j) continue;
                        state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                            i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rowRng);
                    }
                    #pragma omp critical(roomsim_progress)
                    {
                        ++completed;
                        if (completed % 100 == 0 || completed == state.numTriangles) {
                            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", completed, state.numTriangles);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    std::vector<Vec3> centers(state.numTriangles);
    int minDelay = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
        }
        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minDelay)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;
                int delay = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(
                    (centers[i] - centers[j]).norm() * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
                state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = delay;
                minDelay = std::min(minDelay, delay);
            }
        }
    }
    state.minDelay = minDelay;
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    const size_t blockSize = static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay);
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        // Distinct mesh triangles have strictly positive delays. Within a block
        // shorter than the minimum delay, every read comes from an earlier block.
        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < state.numTimesteps; begin += blockSize) {
            const size_t end = std::min(begin + blockSize, state.numTimesteps);
            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (size_t t = begin; t < end; ++t) {
                for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
                    val_t sumB = ZERO;

                    for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                        if (i == j) continue;

                        int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];

                        // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                        if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                        val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                        if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                        // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                        size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                        val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
                        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                        // Keep the original emitter order for identical rounding.
                        sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
                    }

                    // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
                    state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
                }
            }

            // The worksharing barrier above publishes this block's radiosity.
            #pragma omp single nowait
            {
                for (size_t t = begin; t < end; ++t) {
                    if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
                        printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    // Store each triangle's history contiguously instead of repeatedly striding
    // across the entire room for every correlation lag.
    std::vector<val_t> history(state.numTriangles * state.numTimesteps);
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
                history[i * state.numTimesteps + t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)];
            }
        }
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = history[i * state.numTimesteps + tt];
                val_t pS = history[state.sourceIndex * state.numTimesteps + tt - t];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);

    // Check source distance is zero or very small
    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
    }

    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
    int receivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                receivedEnergy++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);

    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
+            centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
         }
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minDelay)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {

+                state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = delay;

+            }
+        }

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u -c '
import os, subprocess, re, difflib
from pathlib import Path
original = \"/**\\n * Room Response Simulation Benchmark\\n * \\n * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response\\n * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light\\n * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:\\n * \\n * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry\\n * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)\\n * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values\\n * \\n * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.\\n */\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <array>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdint>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <map>\\n#include <memory>\\n#include <random>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Types and Constants\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nusing idx_t = uint32_t;\\nusing val_t = float;\\n\\nconstexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;\\nconstexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;\\nconstexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;\\nconstexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)\\nconstexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;\\nconstexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling\\nconstexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;\\nconstexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Vector and Triangle Types\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nstruct Vec3 {\\n    val_t x, y, z;\\n\\n    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}\\n    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\\n    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}\\n\\n    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }\\n    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }\\n    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }\\n    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }\\n    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }\\n\\n    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }\\n    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {\\n        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};\\n    }\\n\\n    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }\\n    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }\\n    Vec3 normalized() const {\\n        val_t n = norm();\\n        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();\\n    }\\n\\n    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {\\n        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;\\n    }\\n};\\n\\nstruct Triangle {\\n    Vec3 a, b, c;\\n    Vec3 _normal;\\n\\n    Triangle() = default;\\n    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)\\n        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}\\n\\n    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }\\n    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }\\n\\n    val_t area() const {\\n        Vec3 ab = b - a;\\n        Vec3 ac = c - a;\\n        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();\\n    }\\n\\n    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {\\n    // Translate triangle to box center\\n    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;\\n    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;\\n    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;\\n\\n    // Triangle edges\\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\\n\\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\\n    };\\n\\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;\\n\\n    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);\\n    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;\\n\\n    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);\\n    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\\n\\n    // Test triangle normal\\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\\n\\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\\n        return "'!(minP > r || maxP < -r);'"\\n    };\\n\\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\\n                if ("'!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;'"\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    return true;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\\n\\nclass Octree {\\npublic:\\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\\n    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles\\n\\n    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}\\n\\n    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {\\n        allTriangles = &triangles;\\n        if (triangles.empty()) return;\\n\\n        // Compute bounding box\\n        minBound = triangles[0].a;\\n        maxBound = triangles[0].a;\\n        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {\\n            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {\\n                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);\\n                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);\\n                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);\\n                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);\\n                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);\\n                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);\\n            }\\n        }\\n\\n        // Collect all indices\\n        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;\\n\\n        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);\\n    }\\n\\nprivate:\\n    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {\\n        minBound = nodeMin;\\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\\n\\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\\n            triangleIndices = indices;\\n            return;\\n        }\\n\\n        // Subdivide into 8 children\\n        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\\n        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\\n\\n        for (size_t idx : indices) {\\n            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\\n\\n            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\\n            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\\n                Vec3 childCenter = center;\\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\\n\\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\\n                }\\n            }\\n        }\\n\\n        // Check if we can'\\''t split further (all triangles in one child)\\n        bool canSplit = false;\\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\\n            if ("'!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {'"\\n                canSplit = true;\\n                break;\\n            }\\n        }\\n\\n        if ("'!canSplit) {'"\\n            triangleIndices = indices;\\n            return;\\n        }\\n\\n        // Build children\\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\\n            if ("'!childIndices[i].empty()) {'"\\n                Vec3 childMin = center;\\n                Vec3 childMax = center;\\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;\\n\\n                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\\n                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\\n                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\npublic:\\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node'\\''s bounding box\\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\\n        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\\n\\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\\n\\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\\n\\n        return true;\\n    }\\n\\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\\n    template<typename Func>\\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\\n        if ("'!triangleIndices.empty()) {'"\\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\\n            }\\n            return false;\\n        }\\n\\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\\nclass IcosphereMesh {\\npublic:\\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\\n\\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\\n\\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\\n        };\\n\\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\\n        };\\n\\n        // Subdivide\\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\\n\\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\\n                if (it "'!= midpointCache.end()) return it->second;'"\\n\\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\\n                return idx;\\n            };\\n\\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\\n\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\\n            }\\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\\n        }\\n\\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \\\"room\\\")\\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\\n        }\\n    }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Random Number Generation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nclass RandomGenerator {\\n    std::mt19937 rng;\\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\\npublic:\\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\\n};\\n\\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\\n        u = 1.0f - u;\\n        v = 1.0f - v;\\n    }\\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\\n\\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\\n\\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\\n\\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\\n        }\\n        return false;\\n    });\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\\n}\\n\\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\\n\\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\\n\\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\\n\\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\\n\\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\\n\\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\\n\\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\\n\\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\\n    }\\n\\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Room Response Simulation State\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nstruct SimulationState {\\n    size_t numTriangles;\\n    size_t numTimesteps;\\n\\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\\n\\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\\n\\n    size_t sourceIndex;\\n\\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Initialization\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\\n    // Generate mesh\\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\\\n\\\", state.numTriangles);\\n\\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\\n    printf(\\\"Building octree...\\\\n\\\");\\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\\n\\n    // Initialize areas\\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\\n    }\\n\\n    // Initialize reflectivity\\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\\n\\n    // Initialize matrices\\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n\\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Precomputation Phase\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\\\n\\\");\\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n            if (i == j) continue;\\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\\n        }\\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\\n            printf(\\\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\\\n\\\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n            if (i == j) continue;\\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\\n\\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n                if (i == j) continue;\\n\\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\n\\n                // Skip if wave hasn'\\''t yet propagated from j to i\\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\\n\\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\\n            }\\n\\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\\n        }\\n\\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\\n            printf(\\\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\\\n\\\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\\n        int bestT = 0;\\n\\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\\n\\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\\n                sum += pS * pB;\\n            }\\n\\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\\n                maxCorr = sum;\\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\\n            }\\n        }\\n\\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Validation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\nValidation:\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\\n        if (d < 0) {\\n            allNonNegative = false;\\n            printf(\\\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\\\n\\\", i, d);\\n        }\\n        if ("'!std::isfinite(d)) {'"\\n            printf(\\\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\\\n\\\", i, d);\\n            return false;\\n        }\\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\\n        sumDist += d;\\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\\n    }\\n\\n    printf(\\\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\\\n\\\", minDist, maxDist);\\n    printf(\\\"  Average distance: %.4f\\\\n\\\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\\n    printf(\\\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\\\n\\\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\\n\\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\\n        printf(\\\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\\\n\\\", srcDist);\\n    }\\n\\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\\n    int +                size_t sampledPairs = 0;
+                const Vec3 normal = state.triangles[i].normal();
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {

codex
The rounding differences came from changing how mesh triangles were constructed. Preserving the original construction restored exact matches for form factors, delays, radiosity, and distances. The parallel simulation also processes independent timesteps in blocks to reduce synchronization; I’m checking edge cases and scaling up to 128 threads.
